### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-001

**检查内容：** In distributed SGD (Algorithm 1), the master updates its iterate according to Equation (2): x̃^{k+1} = x̃^k - α^{k+1} (1/m Σ\_{i=1}^m ∇F(x̃^k, ξ\_i^k) + ∇f\_0(x̃^k)).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Equation (2) and Algorithm 1 line 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:8)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/experiment.py, the `_baseline_step` function (lines 43–71) implements distributed SGD. For method=="sgd": (1) each worker computes `grad = softmax_gradient(x, y, theta, 0.0)` — gradient of loss w.r.t. master parameter `theta` (i.e., ∇F(x̃^k, ξ\_i^k) evaluated at x̃^k), (2) gradients are averaged: `grad = stacked.mean(axis=0)` (line 62) — this is the 1/m Σ∇F term, (3) the update is `theta - alpha * (grad + weight_decay * theta)` (line 71), where `weight_decay * theta` is ∇f\_0(x̃^k) (L2 regularization gradient). In experiment.py line 163, alpha decays as `alpha_base / sqrt(iteration)`, matching α^{k+1}. The full update matches Eq. (2): x̃^{k+1} = x̃^k - α^{k+1}(1/m Σ∇F(x̃^k,ξ\_i^k) + ∇f\_0(x̃^k)).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The RSA algorithm (rsa\_step in rsa.py) uses a different update rule (with proximal penalty terms), but the plain distributed SGD baseline in \_baseline\_step correctly implements Equation (2).

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, the SGD method (lines 189-222) implements the update. Each worker computes `grad = softmax_gradient(x_batch, worker_y[indices], theta, l2)` (line 191), where softmax\_gradient returns `(probs.T @ x)/len(x) + l2*theta` (line 29) — this combines ∇F(x̃^k, ξ\_i^k) with ∇f\_0(x̃^k) = l2\*θ. The master then aggregates: `aggregate = message_array.mean(axis=0)` (line 210) giving 1/m Σ(∇F\_i + l2\*θ) = 1/m Σ∇F\_i + l2\*θ, and applies `theta -= alpha * aggregate` (line 222). This precisely implements x̃^{k+1} = x̃^k - α^{k+1}(1/m Σ∇F(x̃^k,ξ\_i^k) + ∇f\_0(x̃^k)) from Equation (2)/Algorithm 1 line 4, with α = alpha0/sqrt(step+1) (line 169).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The l2 regularization (∇f\_0) is included in each worker's gradient rather than added separately at the master. Mathematically, since mean(l2\*θ) = l2\*θ, the result is equivalent to the paper's formulation where ∇f\_0 is added once at the master step.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-002

**检查内容：** In ℓ1-norm RSA, each regular worker i updates its local iterate according to Equation (7): x\_i^{k+1} = x\_i^k - α^{k+1} (∇F(x\_i^k, ξ\_i^k) + λ sign(x\_i^k - x\_0^k)).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Equation (7) and Algorithm 2 worker line 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:17)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_repro/rsa.py, function rsa\_step (lines ~45-55): For each regular worker i, `next_local[i] = state.locals[i] - alpha * (grad + lam * norm_subgradient(state.locals[i] - state.master, norm))`. When norm="l1", norm\_subgradient returns np.sign(delta) (defined at line 12). This directly implements x\_i^{k+1} = x\_i^k - α(∇F(x\_i^k,ξ\_i^k)/n + λ·sign(x\_i^k - x\_0^k)), matching Equation (7). The gradient is divided by the number of workers (a normalized variant) but the sign penalty term λ·sign(x\_i^k - x\_0^k) is implemented exactly as described.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The gradient is divided by `workers` (batch-normalized), which is a minor implementation detail vs the paper formula, but the overall structure — SGD step minus alpha\*(grad + lambda\*sign(local - master)) — matches Equation (7) for ℓ1-norm RSA.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, for method "rsa\_l1" (p=1), the worker update (around line 176) is: `local[worker_id] -= alpha * (grad / workers + lam * norm_subgradient(local[worker_id] - theta, p))`. With p=1, norm\_subgradient returns np.sign(delta), so this implements: x\_i^{k+1} = x\_i^k - α\*(∇F/m + λ·sign(x\_i^k - x\_0^k)), where x\_0^k is `theta` (the server/master iterate). The sign-based penalty on (local - server) directly matches Eq. (7). The gradient is scaled by 1/workers but the fundamental structure — stochastic gradient plus λ·sign(x\_i - x\_0) penalty — is correctly implemented.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code divides the gradient term by the number of workers (grad/workers) whereas the paper's Eq. (7) shows the raw gradient without this normalization. This is a minor scaling difference but the core algorithmic structure of Eq. (7) is faithfully implemented.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-003

**检查内容：** In ℓ1-norm RSA, the master updates its iterate according to Equation (8): x\_0^{k+1} = x\_0^k - α^{k+1} (∇f\_0(x\_0^k) - λ ( Σ\_{i∈R} sign(x\_i^k - x\_0^k) + Σ\_{j∈B} sign(z\_j^k - x\_0^k) )).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Equation (8) and Algorithm 2 master line 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:26)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:57)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/rsa.py, the rsa\_step function implements the master update at lines 62-65. The penalty is accumulated as `penalty += norm_subgradient(state.master - msg, norm)`, which for l1 norm gives `sign(x_0 - x_i) = -sign(x_i - x_0)`. Then `next_master = state.master - alpha * (weight_decay * state.master + lam * penalty)`. Expanding: `x_0^{k+1} = x_0^k - α*(wd*x_0^k + λ*(-Σ sign(x_i - x_0^k))) = x_0^k - α*∇f_0(x_0^k) + α*λ*Σ sign(x_i - x_0^k)`. This matches Equation (8): `x_0^k - α*(∇f_0(x_0^k) - λ*(Σ sign(x_i^k - x_0^k) + Σ sign(z_j^k - x_0^k)))`. The `weight_decay * state.master` serves as ∇f\_0 (L2 regularization gradient), and received\[\] contains both regular workers and Byzantine workers' messages, covering both Σ\_R and Σ\_B terms.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The master does not compute an explicit data gradient ∇f\_0 beyond L2 weight decay, consistent with many RSA implementations where the master holds no local data. The sign direction convention (using `master - msg` rather than `msg - master`) correctly accounts for the minus in the formula.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:57)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, the RSA master update for l1-norm (method="rsa\_l1") is at lines approx. 194-195: `penalty = np.sum([norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p) for msg in message_array], axis=0)` followed by `theta -= alpha * (l2 * theta + lam * penalty)`. With p=1, `norm_subgradient(theta - msg, 1)` returns `np.sign(theta - msg_i)`, so penalty = Σ\_i sign(x\_0 - x\_i). The update is x\_0 -= α\*(l2\*x\_0 + λ\*Σ sign(x\_0-x\_i)), which is algebraically equivalent to Eq. (8): x\_0 - α\*(∇f\_0(x\_0) - λ\*Σ sign(x\_i - x\_0)) since sign(x\_0-x\_i) = -sign(x\_i-x\_0). The term `l2*theta` serves as ∇f\_0(x\_0^k), and `lam*penalty` correctly implements the −λ(Σ sign(x\_i-x\_0)) subgradient penalty summed over all (regular R and Byzantine B) worker messages.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code uses sign(theta - msg) rather than sign(msg - theta), but because the paper's equation has a negative sign in front of λ(...), these are equivalent. Workers are not explicitly partitioned into R and B sets in the sum; all workers contribute their messages (Byzantine workers contribute corrupted values), which is consistent with the algorithm's design where the sum covers all i∈R∪B.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-005

**检查内容：** In ℓp-norm RSA, the master updates its iterate according to Equation (11): x\_0^{k+1} = x\_0^k - α^{k+1} (∇f\_0(x\_0^k) - λ ( Σ\_{i∈R} ∂\_{x\_0} \|\|x\_i^k - x\_0^k\|\|\_p + Σ\_{j∈B} ∂\_{x\_0} \|\|z\_j^k - x\_0^k\|\|\_p )).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Equation (11) and Algorithm 2 master line 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:44)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:79)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/rsa.py lines 62–65, the master update is:
>   penalty = np.zeros\_like(state.master)
>   for msg in received:
>       penalty += norm\_subgradient(state.master - msg, norm)
>   next\_master = state.master - alpha \* (weight\_decay \* state.master + lam \* penalty)
> 
> This implements Eq. (11): x\_0^{k+1} = x\_0^k - α\*(∇f\_0(x\_0^k) + λ·Σ ∂\_{x\_0}‖x\_0^k − x\_i^k‖\_p).
> The sign is consistent with Eq. (11) because ∂\_{x\_0}‖x\_i−x\_0‖\_p = −∂\_{x\_0}‖x\_0−x\_i‖\_p, so −λ·Σ∂\_{x\_0}‖x\_i−x\_0‖\_p = +λ·Σ norm\_subgradient(master−msg).
> The 'received' array covers both regular workers (indices 0..regular-1) and byzantine workers (indices regular..workers-1), matching Σ\_{i∈R} + Σ\_{j∈B}.
> norm\_subgradient (lines 11–24) implements l1/l2/linf subgradients.
> weight\_decay\*state.master serves as ∇f\_0(x\_0^k) (L2-regularised gradient).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The master gradient ∇f\_0(x\_0^k) uses only weight decay (no data term on master), which may simplify the paper's formulation, but the penalty/penalty-gradient structure of Eq. (11) is faithfully implemented.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:79)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py lines 202-205: `penalty = np.sum([norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p) for msg in message_array], axis=0); theta -= alpha * (l2 * theta + lam * penalty)`. The master iterate `theta` (=x\_0) is updated by subtracting alpha\*(gradient\_of\_f0 + lam\*penalty). Here: (1) `l2 * theta` is ∇f\_0(x\_0^k); (2) `norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p)` = ∂\_{x\_0}\|\|x\_0 - x\_i\|\|\_p; (3) the sum over `message_array` covers all workers (regular R ∪ Byzantine B indices), matching both summation terms in Eq. (11). The sign convention is consistent: ∂\_{x\_0}\|\|x\_i - x\_0\|\|\_p = -∂\_{x\_0}\|\|x\_0 - x\_i\|\|\_p, so −λΣ∂\_{x\_0}\|\|x\_i−x\_0\|\| = +λΣnorm\_subgrad(x\_0−x\_i), matching the code's `+ lam * penalty`. The step size alpha = alpha0/sqrt(step+1) implements α^{k+1}.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The sign algebra requires careful interpretation: the paper writes ∇f\_0 − λΣ∂\_{x\_0}‖x\_i−x\_0‖ which equals ∇f\_0 + λΣ‖x\_0−x\_i‖-subgrad, matching the code's `l2*theta + lam*penalty`.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-006

**检查内容：** For ℓp-norm RSA, the subgradient ∂\|\|x\|\|\_p is given by Proposition 1: ∂\|\|x\|\|\_p = {z ∈ R^d: ⟨z,x⟩ = \|\|x\|\|\_p, \|\|z\|\|\_b ≤ 1}, where 1/b + 1/p = 1.

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Proposition 1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:53)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:90)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/rsa.py, `norm_subgradient` (lines 11-24) handles only three hardcoded string cases: "l1" → np.sign(delta), "l2" → delta/\|\|delta\|\|, "linf" → uniform sign on argmax entries. There is no general ℓp implementation with a float `p` parameter, no dual-norm exponent `b` computed via `1/b + 1/p = 1`, and no code expressing the characterisation ∂\|\|x\|\|\_p = {z: ⟨z,x⟩ = \|\|x\|\|\_p, \|\|z\|\|\_b ≤ 1}. Searches for "lp", "norm\_p", "dual", "holder", "1/p", "p\_norm", "conjugate" across the entire workspace found no relevant matches in the Python source.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The three special cases (l1, l2, linf) are themselves valid subgradients consistent with Proposition 1 for those specific values of p, but the general formula from Proposition 1 for arbitrary p is not implemented.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

For p=1 it returns sign(delta) (dual norm \|\|·\|\|\_∞ ≤ 1, since 1/∞+1/1=1); for p=2 it returns delta/\|\|delta\|\|\_2 (self-dual, 1/2+1/2=1); for p=inf it returns a vector with ±1/\|argmax\| on max-absolute-value indices (dual norm \|\|·\|\|\_1 ≤ 1, since 1/1+1/∞=1). This function is called in the RSA worker update (line ~157: `lam * norm_subgradient(local[worker_id] - theta, p)`) and server update (line ~165: `penalty = np.sum([norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p) for msg in message_array], axis=0)`). Tests in tests/test\_algorithms.py:6 verify the dual norm properties (\|\|z\|\|\_b ≤ 1), directly testing the Proposition 1 characterization.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation selects specific elements from the subdifferential set (not the full set), which is standard practice — a single valid subgradient is sufficient for gradient descent algorithms.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-008

**检查内容：** RSA uses model aggregation: each worker sends its local iterate x\_i^k to the master, and the master broadcasts its iterate x\_0^k to all workers (Algorithm 2).

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2 and Remark 1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:71)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_repro/rsa.py, function rsa\_step() (lines 45-66): (1) Each worker i computes next\_local\[i\] (x\_i^{k+1}) using state.master (x\_0^k) broadcast from master (line 54: norm\_subgradient(state.locals\[i\] - state.master, norm)); (2) Workers' local iterates are collected in `received = next_local.copy()` (line 55); (3) Master aggregates by summing norm\_subgradients of (master - msg) for each received worker iterate (lines 62-64) and updates next\_master (line 65). The RSAState dataclass (lines 39-42) stores both master and locals, confirming the master-worker structure.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> Byzantine workers may send corrupted messages instead of their true local iterates, but the aggregation structure (workers send x\_i^k to master, master broadcasts x\_0^k to workers) matches Algorithm 2 exactly.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:112)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, the RSA training loop (method.startswith("rsa") branch): (1) Each worker computes its local update and sends local\[worker\_id\] (the local iterate) as its message (lines ~167-178: `true = local[worker_id]`, `msg = _attack_message(true, ...)`, appended to `messages`). (2) The master collects all worker messages and uses them: `message_array = np.asarray(messages)`, then `penalty = np.sum([norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p) for msg in message_array], axis=0)` and `theta -= alpha * (l2 * theta + lam * penalty)`. (3) The master iterate `theta` is used by workers in their local updates: `local[worker_id] -= alpha * (grad / workers + lam * norm_subgradient(local[worker_id] - theta, p))`, implementing the broadcast of master iterate x\_0^k to all workers. This directly implements Algorithm 2's communication pattern.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation matches the paper's Algorithm 2 communication structure exactly: workers send local iterates x\_i^k, master broadcasts its iterate x\_0^k (theta) to all workers for the proximity penalty computation.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / alg-009

**检查内容：** The master does not distinguish between regular and Byzantine workers; it uses the same update rule (8) or (11) regardless of whether the received value is x\_i^k or a faulty z\_i^k.

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.565%）。

**论文定位：** Text after equation (8)。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:80)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/rsa.py, the rsa\_step function applies the master update uniformly over all received messages. After Byzantine workers' messages are replaced (lines: `received[i] = attack_message(...)`), the master iterates over ALL messages without distinction: `for msg in received: penalty += norm_subgradient(state.master - msg, norm)`, then applies `next_master = state.master - alpha * (weight_decay * state.master + lam * penalty)`. There is no conditional or filtering on worker index in the master update loop — regular and Byzantine messages are treated identically by the master.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The Byzantine attack is simulated by replacing the content of `received[i]`, but the master's update rule itself applies the same computation to every message regardless of whether it came from a regular or Byzantine worker, consistent with the paper's claim that the master cannot distinguish between them.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:123)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, the master (server) RSA update at ~line 187-190 assembles all worker messages into `message_array = np.asarray(messages)`, then applies `penalty = np.sum([norm_subgradient(theta - msg, p) for msg in message_array], axis=0)` and `theta -= alpha * (l2 * theta + lam * penalty)` uniformly over all entries. Byzantine workers' messages are corrupted by `_attack_message` and stored in `messages` alongside honest workers' messages, but the master loop treats every entry identically — there is no condition or branch that distinguishes faulty messages from honest ones.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> This is an intentional design choice matching the paper: RSA's robustness comes from the norm-subgradient penalty structure, not from the master filtering Byzantine inputs.

However, the paper specifies the coefficient as 0.001 (so 0.001/2 \* \|\|θ\|\|²), while the config configs/exp\_001\_no\_attack.json sets `"l2": 0.01`, making the actual term 0.01/2 \* \|\|θ\|\|² = 0.005 \* \|\|θ\|\|² — 10× larger than the paper's 0.0005 \* \|\|θ\|\|². The default in algorithms.py (line 141) also uses l2=0.01, and run\_experiment.py (line 109) reads this value from config.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The architecture (softmax + ℓ2 regularization) is correct, but the regularization coefficient is 0.01 (code) vs 0.001 (paper), a 10× discrepancy. The rubric specifically states f0(x̃) = 0.001/2 \* \|\|x̃\|\|², which is not what the code implements for exp1\_no\_byzantine\_attacks.

In rsa\_repro/experiment.py (line 121), the MNIST dataset is split into train/test sets (`train_x, train_y, test_x, test_y = load_mnist(...)`), and at line 177, accuracy is evaluated exclusively on the test split: `accs.append(accuracy(test_x, test_y, current))`. The label "top-1 accuracy" is used explicitly in the plot label at line 200: `_plot(out / "accuracy.png", x, acc_series, "top-1 accuracy", ...)`.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

Line 228: called as `accuracy(eval_x, test_y, theta)` using test labels. run\_experiment.py line 169: y-axis explicitly labeled `"test top-1 accuracy"`. Lines 116-122: final and best accuracy from result.accuracy are recorded as the primary metrics per run.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The `accuracy` function uses argmax over softmax predictions compared to ground-truth labels, which is exactly top-1 accuracy. It is evaluated on the held-out test set (test\_x, test\_y from MNIST).

**论文定位：** §Without Byzantine attacks。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:218)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp1\_no\_byzantine\_attacks；数据集=MNIST；方法=RSA；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

```json
  }
}
```

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:222)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In configs/exp\_001.yaml (the exp1\_no\_byzantine\_attacks experiment), the global alpha is 0.003 but rsa\_l1 has a method-specific override: `method_options: rsa_l1: {lambda: 0.1, alpha: 0.001}`. In rsa\_repro/experiment.py line 163, the step size is computed as `alpha = float(options.get("alpha", cfg.get("alpha", 0.003))) / np.sqrt(iteration)`. For rsa\_l1, `options.get("alpha")` returns 0.001, so the actual step size used is 0.001/√k, not 0.003/√k.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The global alpha of 0.003 is overridden by the method-specific alpha of 0.001 for rsa\_l1. The claim that ℓ1-norm RSA uses 0.003/√k is incorrect per the implementation.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:222)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py line 169, the step size schedule is `alpha = alpha0 / np.sqrt(step + 1.0)`, matching the α^k = α0/√k form. However, in configs/exp\_001\_no\_attack.json (the no-Byzantine-attacks experiment), the parameter is `"alpha0": 0.001`, not 0.003 as the rubric requires. The ℓ1-norm RSA method (rsa\_l1) uses the same alpha0 as all other methods in exp\_001 — there is no variant-specific override setting alpha0=0.003 for rsa\_l1.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The step schedule formula is correct (divides by √k), but the coefficient is 0.001, not 0.003 as specified by the rubric claim.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

In run\_experiment.py (line 109), this single alpha0 is passed to run\_training() for every method without per-method tuning. In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py (line 169), all methods use the same schedule: alpha = alpha0 / sqrt(step+1). There is no per-method step size configuration, no sweep or grid-search, and no distinct alpha0 values for Ideal SGD, GeoMed, Krum, and Median.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper claims step sizes are hand-tuned to the best for each benchmark algorithm, but the code applies a uniform alpha0=0.001 to all methods with no evidence of individual tuning.

The configs/exp\_002\_same\_q4.json explicitly states `"description":"IID MNIST same-value attack"` and uses `"partition":"iid"` with the full dataset. No train/test limiting flags were passed, confirming 60,000 training and 10,000 test samples.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The eval\_limit is 2000 (limiting test evaluation per iteration for speed), but the full 10,000-sample test set is loaded into memory — only evaluation is truncated, not the dataset itself.

> rsa\_repro/model.py: softmax\_gradient() implements softmax regression (lines 11-18); cross\_entropy() (line 39) adds `weight_decay * np.sum(theta * theta) / 2.0` which is λ/2 ‖θ‖². All same-value attack configs (configs/exp\_002.yaml, exp\_003.yaml, etc.) set weight\_decay: 0.01, giving the exact regularization term 0.01/2 ‖x̃‖².

Configs exp\_002\_same\_q4.json and exp\_003\_same\_q8.json (same-value attacks on MNIST) both set `"l2": 0.01`, confirming f0(x̃) = 0.01/2 \* \|\|x̃\|\|².

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The regularization is applied as the master regularizer f\_0 per the paper's formulation; a comment in line 180-181 explicitly notes this distinction.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> configs/exp\_002.yaml (same-value attack q=4) and configs/exp\_003.yaml (same-value attack q=8) both set `workers: 20`. The RSAState class in rsa\_repro/rsa.py defines a single `master` parameter (line 41) plus `locals` array of size `workers` (line 150: `np.repeat(theta[None,...], workers, axis=0)`). In rsa\_step(), `next_master` is computed once (lines 62-65) while `next_local[i]` is computed for each of the 20 workers. Run logs confirm: results/formal/exp\_002/run.log: "seed=1812 workers=20 q=4 attack=same\_value" and results/formal/exp\_003/run.log: "seed=1813 workers=20 q=8 attack=same\_value".

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The master is a single parameter vector (not a separate process in the OS sense), but the algorithmic model is explicitly 20 workers + 1 master as described in the paper.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:277)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Configs for exp2 same-value attacks (exp\_002\_same\_q4 and exp\_003\_same\_q8) both set "num\_workers": 20. The algorithm in rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py (lines 158-205) implements a master-worker architecture: `workers = len(partitions)` (=20 workers), each with a local model in `local[worker_id]`, and a single master server maintaining `theta` (line 160) that aggregates messages from all workers (lines 202-223). The "master" process handles the theta update (line 205) while 20 worker processes compute local gradient/model updates and send messages. The comment at line 180-181 explicitly references "master regularizer f\_0". README.md (line 51) also confirms "20-worker" setup.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code simulates the distributed system with 20 workers and 1 master (server) in a single process using a loop, rather than actual separate processes, but the logical architecture with 20 workers + 1 master is clearly implemented as described in the paper.

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:299)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/rsa.py lines 27-31, the `attack_message` function handles same\_value attacks: `if attack == "same_value": return np.full_like(message, value)` with default `value: float = 100.0`. The config configs/exp\_002.yaml sets `attack: same_value` and `attack_value: 100.0` for the same-value attack experiment (exp2 / Figure 4a). Byzantine workers call this function in `rsa_step` (line 61), producing a constant vector filled with 100.0 — matching the claim that vi^k = c·1 with c=100.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The "all-one vector" in the paper corresponds to np.full\_like(message, 100.0), i.e., a vector filled with 100 — semantically equivalent to c·1 where c=100 and 1 is the all-ones vector.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:299)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py (line 91-112), the function `_attack_message` handles the "same" attack by returning `np.full_like(true_value, same_value)` — an all-ones-like vector filled with `same_value` (default 100.0). The configs for same-value experiments explicitly set `"attack":"same"` and `"same_value":100.0` (configs/exp\_002\_same\_q4.json: `{"attack":"same","same_value":100.0,...}` and configs/exp\_003\_same\_q8.json: `{"attack":"same","same_value":100.0,...}`). Byzantine workers (those with `worker_id >= workers - num_byzantine`) call `_attack_message` with this attack type, producing a vector filled with c=100 as described in the paper.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The vector produced is `np.full_like(true_value, 100.0)`, which fills with the scalar value 100 — equivalent to `c * 1` where `1` is an all-ones vector and `c=100`.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / setup-023

**检查内容：** Softmax regression is used with an ℓ2-norm regularization term f\_0(˜x) = 0.01/2 \|\|˜x\|\|^2.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.565%）。

**论文定位：** §Numerical Tests。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:485)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp3\_sign\_flipping\_attacks；数据集=MNIST；方法=all；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

```json

```

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:387)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Softmax regression is implemented in rsa\_repro/model.py (softmax\_gradient and cross\_entropy functions). The ℓ2 regularization appears in cross\_entropy(): `loss + weight_decay * np.sum(theta * theta) / 2.0`, which matches f\_0(x̃) = λ/2 \|\|x̃\|\|². The sign-flipping experiment configs (configs/exp\_004.yaml and configs/exp\_005.yaml) both set `weight_decay: 0.01`, giving exactly f\_0(x̃) = 0.01/2 \|\|x̃\|\|². The gradient in softmax\_gradient also adds `weight_decay * theta` when weight\_decay != 0. Both sign-flipping configs cover all the methods listed (ideal\_sgd, sgd, geomed, krum, median, rsa).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper refers to the parameter as applying to the model parameter θ (called x̃ in the paper's notation); the code uses `theta` and `weight_decay`. The functional form is identical.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:387)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py: softmax\_prob (line 18), softmax\_gradient (line 26-29), and cross\_entropy (line 36-38) implement softmax regression. The cross\_entropy function returns `-log(probs).mean() + 0.5 * l2 * np.sum(theta * theta)`, which is exactly f\_0 = (l2/2)\|\|θ\|\|^2. The l2 parameter defaults to 0.01 (algorithms.py line 141) and is explicitly set to "l2": 0.01 in all sign-flipping configs: configs/exp\_004\_sign\_q4.json and configs/exp\_005\_sign\_q8.json. run\_experiment.py line 109 passes `l2=float(config.get("l2", 0.01))` to the training loop. The gradient also adds the regularization term: `l2 * theta` (line 29).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The formula 0.5 \* l2 \* \|\|theta\|\|^2 with l2=0.01 is algebraically identical to 0.01/2 \* \|\|x̃\|\|^2 as stated in the paper.

In rsa\_repro/experiment.py line 163, the step size is computed as: alpha = float(options.get("alpha", cfg.get("alpha", 0.003))) / np.sqrt(iteration), which evaluates to 0.001/sqrt(k) for this config. The rsa\_step function in rsa\_repro/rsa.py then uses this alpha for the ℓ1-norm RSA update.

In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py line 169, the step size is computed as `alpha = alpha0 / np.sqrt(step + 1.0)`, giving α^k = 0.002/√k. The RSA-ℓ1 method (method=="rsa\_l1", p=1) uses this same schedule, confirmed at lines 203–205.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code uses 0-indexed steps with +1 offset (sqrt(step+1)), which is equivalent to 1-indexed k: α^k = 0.002/√k as stated in the rubric.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In configs/exp\_006\_gaussian\_q4.json and exp\_007\_gaussian\_q8.json, a single "alpha0": 0.002 is defined and applied to ALL methods (GeoMed, Krum, Median, SGD, and RSA variants). The run\_training() function in rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py accepts a single alpha0 parameter and computes alpha = alpha0 / sqrt(step + 1) uniformly for every method — there is no per-method step size. Neither the config files nor the code support method-specific step sizes.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The claim requires that each benchmark algorithm has an individually hand-tuned (best) step size. The implementation uses one shared alpha0 for all algorithms in each experiment, making per-algorithm tuning impossible.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / setup-044

**检查内容：** The model is softmax regression with an ℓ2-norm regularization term f0(˜x) = 0.2 01 ∥x˜∥2.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** §Numerical Tests。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:851)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp5\_runtime\_comparison；数据集=MNIST；方法=all；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp5_runtime_comparison",
    "method": "all",
    "dataset": "MNIST"
  }
}
```

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:618)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_repro/model.py: `softmax_gradient()` implements softmax regression with ℓ2 weight-decay term `grad + weight_decay * theta`, and `cross_entropy()` adds `weight_decay * np.sum(theta * theta) / 2.0` as the regularizer. All experiment configs (exp\_001.yaml through exp\_010.yaml) set `weight_decay: 0.01`, matching the paper's ℓ2 coefficient. The runtime comparison experiment (exp\_003.yaml/exp\_005.yaml) uses the same softmax+ℓ2 model on MNIST. The experiment labeled `exp5_runtime_comparison` in the rubric maps to exp\_003 config (q=8 same-value runtime) which also uses softmax regression with weight\_decay=0.01.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The rubric's `exp5_runtime_comparison` ID doesn't literally match any config (repo uses exp\_003 for runtime); the paper's `f0(x̃) = 0.2 · 0.01 · ‖x̃‖²` notation is ambiguous but weight\_decay=0.01 in `loss + 0.01*‖θ‖²/2` is the implemented ℓ2 regularization. Softmax regression model on MNIST with ℓ2 regularization is confirmed implemented.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:618)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The code implements softmax regression with L2 regularization in rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py (cross\_entropy uses `0.5 * l2 * np.sum(theta * theta)`, and softmax\_gradient adds `l2 * theta`). However, all configs set `"l2": 0.01`, not 0.2. The regularization coefficient does not match the paper's f0(˜x) = 0.2·(1/n)·‖x̃‖². Additionally, the term is implemented as 0.5·l2·‖θ‖² (not normalized by 1/n explicitly). Every experiment config (exp\_001 through exp\_009) uses `"l2": 0.01`.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The model architecture (softmax regression with L2 reg) is present, but the specific coefficient value 0.2 from the paper's f0 term is not used anywhere — 0.01 is used instead. This is a mismatch with the rubric's claim.

In algorithms.py (line 169), the step size alpha = alpha0 / sqrt(step+1) is computed identically for every method with no per-method differentiation. There is no mechanism (no per-method alpha, no grid search script, no tuning config) to set different or individually-optimized step sizes for Ideal SGD, GeoMed, Krum, or Median.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper claims benchmark step sizes are hand-tuned to the best for fair comparison. This repository uses a single shared alpha0 for all methods within each experiment, with no per-method tuning.

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:739)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The model IS softmax regression with ℓ2 regularization — `rsa_repro/model.py` line 39 computes `loss + weight_decay * np.sum(theta * theta) / 2.0`, i.e., f0(x̃) = (weight\_decay/2)‖x̃‖². However, the regularization coefficient is NOT 0.001. All experiment configs (including exp\_006.yaml, exp\_007.yaml, exp\_008.yaml — the closest to "exp6\_impact\_of\_lambda") use `weight_decay: 0.01`, giving f0(x̃) = (0.01/2)‖x̃‖², not (0.001/2)‖x̃‖² as the rubric claims. No config or code path sets weight\_decay to 0.001 for MNIST softmax experiments.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The model architecture and regularization form are correct, but the coefficient is 0.01 rather than the claimed 0.001.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:739)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The repository implements softmax regression (rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py: softmax\_prob, softmax\_gradient, cross\_entropy functions) with an ℓ2 regularization term. The master update uses `theta -= alpha * (l2 * theta + ...)` and cross\_entropy uses `0.5 * l2 * sum(theta^2)`, consistent with (l2/2)\|\|theta\|\|^2. However, ALL configs set "l2": 0.01, not 0.001. The experiment closest to exp6\_impact\_of\_lambda (configs/exp\_008\_lambda\_sweep.json) explicitly sets "l2": 0.01. The code default in run\_training() (line ~140) also specifies `l2: float = 0.01`. No config or code path uses l2=0.001 for the regularization coefficient.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The model structure (softmax regression + ℓ2 regularization) matches, but the coefficient is 0.01 not 0.001, so the exact formula f0(x̃) = (0.001/2)\|\|x̃\|\|^2 is not implemented.

The master's theta is updated at lines 204-205/222. run\_experiment.py line 169 labels the y-axis "test top-1 accuracy". configs/exp\_008\_lambda\_sweep.json confirms this experiment uses RSA on MNIST.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The experiment in the repo is labeled exp\_008\_lambda\_sweep but maps to the paper's exp6\_impact\_of\_lambda. The accuracy function uses argmax (top-1) on test data with the master model theta.

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:871)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_repro/model.py implements softmax regression with ℓ2 regularization (gradient: `grad + weight_decay * theta`, loss: `weight_decay * sum(theta^2) / 2`). However, all configs set `weight_decay: 0.01` — specifically configs/exp\_008.yaml (the closest match to exp7\_rsa\_different\_norms) and all other experiment configs. The paper claims the coefficient should be 0.001/2 (i.e., weight\_decay=0.001 in gradient terms), but the implementation uses weight\_decay=0.01 — 10× larger than the claimed value.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The model type (softmax regression) and regularization type (ℓ2) are correctly implemented, but the specific coefficient 0.001/2 does not match — all configs use weight\_decay=0.01 instead of weight\_decay=0.001.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:871)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The code in rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py implements softmax regression with ℓ2 regularization: cross\_entropy (line 38) includes `0.5 * l2 * np.sum(theta * theta)` and the gradient (line 29) includes `l2 * theta`. However, all experiment configs (exp\_001 through exp\_009) set `"l2": 0.01`, making the effective regularization coefficient `0.5 * 0.01 = 0.005`. The paper specifies `(0.001/2)||x̃||^2 = 0.0005 * ||x̃||^2`, which is 10× smaller than what the configs implement. No config sets `l2=0.001` (which combined with the 0.5 factor would yield the paper's value).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The softmax regression model and ℓ2 regularization structure are correctly implemented, but the regularization strength is 0.005 (code) vs 0.0005 (paper). The experiment exp7\_rsa\_different\_norms is not explicitly named in the repo; the closest experiments use the same l2=0.01 setting.

**检查内容：** Softmax regression uses an ℓ2-norm regularization term f0(x̃) = 0.01/2 \|\|x̃\|\|^2.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Numerical Tests section, first paragraph。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:1599)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_repro/model.py, cross\_entropy() returns `loss + weight_decay * np.sum(theta * theta) / 2.0` and softmax\_gradient() adds `weight_decay * theta` to the gradient — exactly the ℓ2 regularization term (weight\_decay/2)\*\|\|θ\|\|^2. All experiment configs including exp\_008.yaml (the heterogeneous MNIST experiment) set `weight_decay: 0.01`, giving f0(x̃) = 0.01/2 \* \|\|x̃\|\|^2.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The parameter is named `weight_decay` in code but corresponds to the paper's regularization coefficient λ\_reg = 0.01.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1091)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py line 141: default `l2: float = 0.01`; line 38: cross\_entropy includes `+ 0.5 * l2 * np.sum(theta * theta)` — i.e. 0.01/2 \* \|\|θ\|\|^2; line 26-29: softmax\_gradient adds `l2 * theta`. configs/exp\_009\_heterogeneous.json explicitly sets `"l2": 0.01` for the heterogeneous MNIST experiment.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The regularization is applied on the master model parameter θ (denoted x̃ in the paper). The formula 0.5 \* l2 \* \|\|θ\|\|² with l2=0.01 matches f0(x̃) = 0.01/2 \* \|\|x̃\|\|² exactly.

The heterogeneous data experiments (exp\_009/exp\_010 via configs/exp\_009.yaml, configs/exp\_010.yaml with partition=heterogeneous) use the same code path, and results/formal/exp\_009/metrics.json stores per-method "accuracy" trajectory arrays confirming the metric is recorded.

### 1811.03761 / midloop / setup-089

**检查内容：** Each Byzantine worker transmits v\_i^k = v\_r^k, where r=1 (a regular worker).

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.130%）。

**论文定位：** Heterogeneous Data paragraph。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_1811_03761/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:1633)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp8\_heterogeneous\_data；数据集=MNIST heterogeneous；方法=all；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp8_heterogeneous_data",
    "method": "all",
    "dataset": "MNIST heterogeneous",
    "variant": "attack_type=copy_regular_worker_1"
  }
}
```

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1113)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> rsa\_repro/experiment.py line 56-59: `if attack == "heterogeneous_copy" and q:` block with comment "mirroring the paper's v\_i^k = v\_r^k attack" copies worker index 0 (=regular worker 1, 1-based) to all Byzantine workers: `stacked[len(batches) - q :] = stacked[int(attack_kwargs.get("copy_index", 0))]`. Also rsa\_repro/rsa.py line 57-58: `if attack == "heterogeneous_copy": received[regular:] = next_local[hetero_transmit_index]`. Configs exp\_009.yaml and exp\_010.yaml set `hetero_transmit_index: 0` and `attack: heterogeneous_copy`, confirming the copy-from-worker-1 attack. experiment\_id exp\_009/exp\_010 correspond to heterogeneous data experiments.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The rubric references exp8\_heterogeneous\_data but the relevant configs are exp\_009.yaml and exp\_010.yaml (both using attack: heterogeneous\_copy with hetero\_transmit\_index: 0). The implementation correctly sets each Byzantine worker's message to worker r=1 (0-indexed as 0).

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu/agent_reproduction/runs/1811.03761/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1113)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In rsa\_reproduction/algorithms.py, the `_attack_message` function (lines 91-112) implements attack="transmit" by returning `base.copy()` where `base = reference` when reference is provided. For RSA (line 185), `reference = local[0]` — the first regular worker's local model. For gradient aggregators (line 196), `reference = true_messages[0]` — the first regular worker's gradient. The comment at lines 193-195 explicitly states "A transmit attacker replays the first regular worker's message." The exp\_009\_heterogeneous config (configs/exp\_009\_heterogeneous.json) uses `"attack":"transmit"` on MNIST with heterogeneous partition, confirming this is the experiment variant described as attack\_type=copy\_regular\_worker\_1.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The rubric uses 1-indexed r=1 which corresponds to array index 0 (local\[0\] / true\_messages\[0\]) in the code — the first regular worker.

### 1811.03761 / bigloop / res-001

**检查内容：** RSA and GeoMed achieve top-1 accuracy close to Ideal SGD.

GeoMed is within 0.0029 of Ideal SGD; RSA is within 0.0097 of Ideal SGD. **证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/exp\_001\_no\_attack/metrics.csv: In the no-attack configuration on MNIST, averaged over 3 seeds: Ideal SGD final\_accuracy=0.9202, RSA (L1) final\_accuracy=0.9194 (diff=0.0007), GeoMed final\_accuracy=0.9194 (diff=0.0008). RSA (L2) also close at 0.9164 (diff=0.0038). Both RSA (L1) and GeoMed achieve top-1 accuracy within ~0.1 percentage points of Ideal SGD.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> RSA has multiple variants in the outputs (rsa\_l1, rsa\_l2, rsa\_linf). The rsa\_linf variant performs poorly (~0.52), but rsa\_l1 and rsa\_l2 both match SGD closely. The claim is supported by rsa\_l1 (and rsa\_l2). rsa\_linf is an outlier but not the primary RSA variant expected to match SGD.

### 1811.03761 / bigloop / res-008
